Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: 1. rw_lab.exe (PID 7768) runs from cmd.exe (E02). 2. File encryption to *.locked and deletion (E03). 3. Ransom note creation (E04). 4. vssadmin.exe attempt (E05). 5. No persistence (E06). 6. No network activity (E07). Unique defects: 1. Missing hash evidence in chunk_10. 2. Coarse timeline in chunk_5. 3. Placeholder in chunk_2 (sample_rw.exe). 4. Unresolved evidence in chunk_7 (inferred behavior). 5. Missing environmental outcome in chunk_4.
